While existing research has demonstrated the viability of the SAE in interpreting text-based Large Language Models (LLMs), there are no equivalent studies that demonstrate the application of a SAE to audio processing models like Automatic Speech Recognizers (ASRs). In this work, a SAE is applied to Whisper, a Transformer-based ASR, training a high-dimensional sparse latent space on frame-level embeddings extracted from the Whisper encoder. Our work uncovers diverse monosemantic features across linguistic and non-linguistic boundaries, and demonstrates cross-lingual feature steering. This work establishes the viability of a SAE model and demonstrates that Whisper encodes a rich amount of linguistic information.

</details>


### [109] [Combining On-Policy Optimization and Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12227)
*Miguel Moura Ramos,Duarte M. Alves,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: dGRPO：结合GRPO策略优化和蒸馏引导的长上下文对齐方法，在LongBlocks数据集上展现更稳定有效的长上下文推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM适应方法存在局限性：1) 离线策略方法（SFT、KD）存在暴露偏差，长序列中模型生成错误难以恢复；2) 在线策略强化学习方法（GRPO）训练与模型生成状态更对齐，但稀疏奖励导致不稳定和采样效率低；3) 在线蒸馏方法（OPD）提供密集标记级引导，但不能直接优化任意奖励信号

Method: 提出蒸馏组相对策略优化（dGRPO）：在GRPO基础上通过OPD从更强教师模型获取密集引导，同时引入LongBlocks合成数据集涵盖多跳推理、上下文基础理解、长文本生成任务，通过实验比较离线策略训练、稀疏奖励GRPO和组合方法

Result: 结果表明，将基于结果的策略优化与知识蒸馏结合在单一目标中，为长上下文推理提供了更稳定有效的路径，同时保持了短上下文能力

Conclusion: dGRPO方法通过结合组相对策略优化的优势与蒸馏的密集引导，克服了现有方法在长上下文适应中的局限性，提供了改进的长上下文对齐方案

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to long-context tasks requires post-training methods that remain accurate and coherent over thousands of tokens. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align training with model-generated states, but are unstable and sample-inefficient due to sparse rewards; 3) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance, but does not directly optimize arbitrary reward signals. In this paper, we propose Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO), a method for long-context reasoning that augments GRPO with dense guidance from a stronger teacher via OPD. We also introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic long-context dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. We conduct extensive experiments and ablations comparing off-policy training, sparse-reward GRPO, and our combined approach, leading to an improved recipe for long-context alignment. Overall, our results show that combining outcome-based policy optimization with knowledge distillation in a single objective provides a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning, while preserving short-context capabilities.

</details>


### [110] [Mind the Pause: Disfluency-Aware Objective Tuning for Multilingual Speech Correction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12242)
*Deepak Kumar,Baban Gain,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合序列标注和指令微调大语言模型的多语言言语不流畅修正方法，通过对比学习惩罚不流畅标记的重现，在印度语言中实现了优于基准模型的修正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR转录文本中的言语不流畅现象（如填充词、重复、错误起始）会降低可读性并影响下游应用（如聊天机器人、语音助手）的可靠性。现有方法主要关注不流畅标记的检测和移除，但这种方法往往破坏语法结构和语义连贯性，导致句子不完整或不自然。虽然最近有研究探索使用大语言模型，但这些工作主要侧重于不流畅检测或数据增强，而非全面修正。

Method: 提出一个多语言修正流程：1）首先使用序列标注器标记不流畅标记；2）利用这些信号指导大语言模型进行指令微调，将转录文本重写为流畅文本；3）添加对比学习目标，惩罚不流畅标记的再现，鼓励模型在移除不流畅伪影的同时保持语法和意义完整性。

Result: 在三种印度语言（印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语）上的实验显示，该方法相对于强大的基线模型（包括多语言序列到序列模型）取得了持续改进。结果表明仅靠检测策略是不够的，需要结合标记级线索、指令微调和对比学习才能提供有效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 结合标记级信号、指令微调和对比学习的方法为语音驱动的NLP系统中的多语言不流畅修正提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。该方法强调了检测与修正相结合的重要性，而非仅关注检测。代码已公开提供。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts often contain disfluencies, such as fillers, repetitions, and false starts, which reduce readability and hinder downstream applications like chatbots and voice assistants. If left unaddressed, such disfluencies can significantly degrade the reliability of downstream systems. Most existing approaches rely on classical models that focus on identifying disfluent tokens for removal. While this strategy is effective to some extent, it often disrupts grammatical structure and semantic coherence, leading to incomplete or unnatural sentences. Recent literature explored the use of large language models (LLMs); however, these efforts have primarily focused on disfluency detection or data augmentation, rather than performing comprehensive correction. We propose a multilingual correction pipeline where a sequence tagger first marks disfluent tokens, and these signals guide instruction fine-tuning of an LLM to rewrite transcripts into fluent text. To further improve reliability, we add a contrastive learning objective that penalizes the reproduction of disfluent tokens, encouraging the model to preserve grammar and meaning while removing disfluent artifacts. Our experiments across three Indian languages, namely Hindi, Bengali, and Marathi show consistent improvements over strong baselines, including multilingual sequence-to-sequence models. These results highlight that detection-only strategies are insufficient. Combining token-level cues with instruction tuning and contrastive learning provides a practical and scalable solution for multilingual disfluency correction in speech-driven NLP systems. We make the codes publicly available at https://github.com/deepak-kumar-98/Mind-the-Pause.

</details>


### [111] [PreScam: A Benchmark for Predicting Scam Progression from Early Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12243)
*Weixiang Sun,Shang Ma,Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Colby Nelson,Xusheng Xiao,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PreScam是一个用于建模真实世界对话式诈骗演进过程的基准测试，包含11,573个诈骗对话实例，基准测试模型在实时终止预测和诈骗者行为预测两个任务上的表现，结果显示当前模型能捕捉部分诈骗线索但难以跟踪风险升级过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式诈骗(如浪漫诈骗、投资诈骗)已成为主要的在线欺诈形式。现有研究主要关注静态诈骗检测或合成诈骗，而缺乏对真实世界诈骗如何在多轮对话中随时间演进的理解。需要评估语言模型是否能够理解真实诈骗的演进过程。

Method: 构建PreScam基准：从用户提交的诈骗报告中筛选结构化177,989份原始报告，形成11,573个对话式诈骗实例，涵盖20种诈骗类别。采用提出的诈骗杀伤链定义诈骗生命周期，对每个实例进行分层结构组织，并在话轮级别标注诈骗者心理行为和受害者响应。基准测试两个任务：实时终止预测(判断对话是否接近终止阶段)和诈骗者行为预测(预测诈骗者后续行为)。

Result: 结果显示表面流畅性与演进建模之间存在明显差距：有监督编码器在实时终止预测任务上显著优于零样本LLM，而即使是强LLM在下一行为预测任务上也只有中等成功率。模型能够捕捉部分诈骗相关线索，但在跟踪风险如何升级和操纵如何跨越话轮展开方面仍有困难。

Conclusion: PreScam基准为理解真实世界对话式诈骗的演进过程提供了重要工具。研究发现当前模型在诈骗演进建模方面存在局限性，强调需要更深入地理解诈骗动态过程，而非仅仅依赖表面特征检测。这为未来诈骗检测和预防系统的发展提供了方向。

Abstract: Conversational scams, such as romance and investment scams, are emerging as a major form of online fraud. Unlike one-shot scam lures such as fake lottery or unpaid toll messages, they unfold through multi-turn conversations in which scammers gradually manipulate victims using evolving psychological techniques. However, existing research mainly focuses on static scam detection or synthetic scams, leaving open whether language models can understand how real-world scams progress over time. We introduce PreScam, a benchmark for modeling scam progression from early conversations. Built from user-submitted scam reports, PreScam filters and structures 177,989 raw reports into 11,573 conversational scam instances spanning 20 scam categories. Each instance is hierarchically structured according to the scam lifecycle defined by the proposed scam kill chain, and further annotated at the turn level with scammer psychological actions and victim responses. We benchmark models on two tasks: real-time termination prediction, which estimates whether a conversation is approaching the termination stage, and scammer action prediction, which forecasts the scammer's subsequent actions. Results show a clear gap between surface-level fluency and progression modeling: supervised encoders substantially outperform zero-shot LLMs on real-time termination prediction, while next-action prediction remains only moderately successful even for strong LLMs. Taken together, these results show that current models can capture some scam-related cues, yet still struggle to track how risk escalates and how manipulation unfolds across turns.

</details>


### [112] [What makes a word hard to learn? Modeling L1 influence on English vocabulary difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12281)
*Jonas Mayer Martins,Zhuojing Huang,Aaricia Herygers,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过梯度提升模型计算英语词汇学习难度，分析不同母语背景学习者（西班牙语、德语、汉语）的词汇习得难点，发现词汇熟悉度是共同主导因素，但西班牙语和德语学习者还受正字法迁移影响，而汉语学习者则主要依赖熟悉度和表层特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究词汇学习难度的决定因素，以及这些因素如何因学习者的母语不同而变化。具体探究英语学习者（母语为西班牙语、德语或汉语）在词汇习得过程中面临的挑战，旨在为个性化词汇教学课程设计提供依据。

Method: 使用梯度提升模型（gradient-boosted models）训练预测词汇难度，特征包括：词汇熟悉度（如词频）、语义特征、表层形式特征和跨语言迁移特征。针对母语为西班牙语、德语和汉语的三组英语学习者分别建模。采用Shapley值分析方法确定各特征组的重要性。

Result: 1. 词汇熟悉度是所有三组学习者共同的主导特征组。2. 西班牙语和德语学习者的预测还依赖于正字法迁移特征。3. 汉语学习者由于缺乏正字法迁移机制，其词汇难度主要由熟悉度和表层特征共同决定。4. 模型提供了可解释的、针对特定母语背景的难度评估。

Conclusion: 研究开发了基于母语背景的词汇难度预测模型，揭示了跨语言迁移在词汇习得中的重要性，特别是正字法迁移对具有字母文字背景学习者的促进作用。这些模型可为设计个性化词汇教学课程提供科学依据，帮助教师根据学习者母语特点优化教学策略。

Abstract: What makes a word difficult to learn, and how does the difficulty depend on the learner's native language? We computationally model vocabulary difficulty for English learners whose first language is Spanish, German, or Chinese with gradient-boosted models trained on features related to a word's familiarity (e.g., frequency), meaning, surface form, and cross-linguistic transfer. Using Shapley values, we determine the importance of each feature group. Word familiarity is the dominant feature group shared by all three languages. However, predictions for Spanish- and German-speaking learners rely additionally on orthographic transfer. This transfer mechanism is unavailable to Chinese learners, whose difficulty is shaped by a combination of familiarity and surface features alone. Our models provide interpretable, L1-tailored difficulty estimates that can be used to design vocabulary curricula.

</details>


### [113] [TokenRatio: Principled Token-Level Preference Optimization via Ratio Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12288)
*Truong Nguyen,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Khoa Doan,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TBPO是一种基于令牌级别的偏好优化方法，通过引入Bregman散度密度比匹配目标，在保持DPO简洁性的同时，从序列级成对比较中恢复令牌级偏好最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO虽然广泛用于对齐语言模型，但它在全序列级别建模偏好，而生成实际上是基于每个令牌的决策。现有的令牌级扩展方法通常只是在时间步上分解序列级Bradley-Terry目标，未能明确实现每个前缀（状态级别）的最优性。

Method: 提出了令牌级Bregman偏好优化(TBPO)：1) 假设基于前缀的条件下一个令牌动作的令牌级Bradley-Terry偏好模型；2) 推导出Bregman散度密度比匹配目标，该目标推广了逻辑/DPO损失；3) 引入两种实现：TBPO-Q（显式学习轻量级状态基线）和TBPO-A（通过优势归一化移除基线）。

Result: 在指令跟随、有帮助性/无害性和摘要生成等基准测试中，TBPO相对于强序列级和令牌级基线方法，提高了对齐质量、训练稳定性，并增加了输出多样性。

Conclusion: TBPO方法成功地从序列级成对比较中恢复令牌级偏好最优性，在保持DPO简洁性的同时，提供了更精确的偏好建模框架，并在多个对齐任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used RL-free method for aligning language models from pairwise preferences, but it models preferences over full sequences even though generation is driven by per-token decisions. Existing token-level extensions typically decompose a sequence-level Bradley-Terry objective across timesteps, leaving per-prefix (state-wise) optimality implicit. We study how to recover token-level preference optimality using only standard sequence-level pairwise comparisons. We introduce Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization (TBPO), which posits a token-level Bradley-Terry preference model over next-token actions conditioned on the prefix, and derive a Bregman-divergence density-ratio matching objective that generalizes the logistic/DPO loss while preserving the optimal policy induced by the token-level model and maintaining DPO-like simplicity. We introduce two instantiations: TBPO-Q, which explicitly learns a lightweight state baseline, and TBPO-A, which removes the baseline through advantage normalization. Across instruction following, helpfulness/harmlessness, and summarization benchmarks, TBPO improves alignment quality and training stability and increases output diversity relative to strong sequence-level and token-level baselines.

</details>


### [114] [GKnow: Measuring the Entanglement of Gender Bias and Factual Gender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12299)
*Leonor Veloso,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 开发了一个名为GKnow的基准测试来评估语言模型中的性别知识和性别偏见，发现性别偏见和事实性别知识在神经元和回路层面严重交织，神经元消融不是可靠的去偏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么局限于特定性别相关任务（如代词预测），要么未能区分事实性别输出（基于语义属性的正确性别假设）和性别偏见输出（基于刻板印象）。需要更好的方法来评估语言模型中的性别知识和偏见。

Method: 开发GKnow基准测试，用于评估语言模型中不同类型的性别相关预测。通过识别和分析负责性别预测的回路和单个神经元，测试神经元消融对分离刻板印象和事实性别的影响。

Result: 结果显示性别偏见和事实性别知识在回路和神经元层面严重交织。神经元消融不是可靠的去偏方法，而且现有的性别偏见评估基准可能会掩盖神经元消融导致的事实性别知识下降。

Conclusion: 性别偏见和事实性别知识的交织使得简单的神经元消融不可靠。GKnow基准的提出有助于持续开发更稳健的性别偏见评估方法。

Abstract: Recent works have analyzed the impact of individual components of neural networks on gendered predictions, often with a focus on mitigating gender bias. However, mechanistic interpretations of gender tend to (i) focus on a very specific gender-related task, such as gendered pronoun prediction, or (ii) fail to distinguish between the production of factually gendered outputs (the correct assumption of gender given a word that carries gender as a semantic property) and gender biased outputs (based on a stereotype). To address these issues, we curate \gknow, a benchmark to assess gender knowledge and gender bias in language models across different types of gender-related predictions. \gknow allows us to identify and analyze circuits and individual neurons responsible for gendered predictions. We test the impact of neuron ablation on benchmarks for disentangling stereotypical and factual gender (DiFair and the test set of GKnow), as well as StereoSet. Results show that gender bias and factual gender are severely entangled on the level of both circuits and neurons, entailing that ablation is an unreliable debiasing method. Furthermore, we show that benchmarks for evaluating gender bias can hide the decrease in factual gender knowledge that accompanies neuron ablation. We curate GKnow as a contribution to the continuous development of robust gender bias benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [Overview of the MedHopQA track at BioCreative IX: track description, participation and evaluation of systems for multi-hop medical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12313)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Jongmyung Jung,Hyeongsoon Hwang,Quoc-An Nguyen,Hoang-Quynh Le,Harikrishnan Gurushankar Saisudha,Ganesh Chandrasekar,Rustam R. Taktashov,Nadezhda Yu. Bizyukova,Sofia I. R. Conceição,Paulo R. C. Lopes,Reem Abdel Salam,Mary Adewunmi,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioCreative IX MedHopQA共享任务构建了包含1000个具有挑战性的医学多跳问答对的基准数据集，旨在评估大语言模型在生物医学领域的多跳推理能力。最佳系统在MedCPT评估指标上达到89.30%的F1分数，显著优于零样本基线的67.40%。研究发现检索增强生成（RAG）策略对优秀性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域的多跳问答仍然面临重大挑战，需要系统整合多个来源的信息来回答复杂问题。当前的系统在跨文档信息整合和多步推理方面存在不足，特别是在涉及罕见疾病、基因和化学物质等专业领域时。

Method: 研究团队开发了包含1000个挑战性问答对的新数据集，涵盖疾病、基因和化学物质，特别关注罕见疾病。每个问题都需要通过整合两个不同维基百科页面的信息进行两跳推理。设计了BioCreative IX MedHopQA共享任务，吸引了13个团队的48份提交。使用表面字符串比较和概念准确性（MedCPT分数）两种方法评估系统性能。

Result: 结果显示基线大语言模型与增强系统之间存在显著的性能差距。排名第一的提交在MedCPT指标上达到89.30%的F1分数和87.30%的精确匹配分数，而零样本基线的相应分数分别为67.40%和60.20%。研究发现检索增强生成和相关检索策略对获得优秀性能至关重要，概念级评估在正确答案表面形式不同时能改进答案评估。

Conclusion: MedHopQA数据集为生物医学多跳推理研究提供了重要基准。检索增强生成方法在该任务中表现出关键作用，概念级评估比单纯的字面匹配更有效。该数据集已公开可用，支持该重要领域的持续进展。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering (QA) remains a significant challenge in the biomedical domain, requiring systems to integrate information across multiple sources to answer complex questions. To address this problem, the BioCreative IX MedHopQA shared task was designed to benchmark in multi-hop reasoning for large language models (LLMs). We developed a novel dataset of 1,000 challenging QA pairs spanning diseases, genes, and chemicals, with particular emphasis on rare diseases. Each question was constructed to require two-hop reasoning through the integration of information from two distinct Wikipedia pages. The challenge attracted 48 submissions from 13 teams. Systems were evaluated using both surface string comparison and conceptual accuracy (MedCPT score). The results showed a substantial performance gap between baseline LLMs and enhanced systems. The top-ranked submission achieved an 89.30% F1 score on the MedCPT metric and an 87.30% exact match (EM) score, compared with 67.40% and 60.20%, respectively, for the zero-shot baseline. A central finding of the challenge was that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and related retrieval-based strategies were critical for strong performance. In addition, concept-level evaluation improved answer assessment when correct responses differed in surface form. The MedHopQA dataset is publicly available to support continued progress in this important area. Challenge materials: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/research/bionlp/medhopqa and benchmark https://www.codabench.org/competitions/7609/

</details>


### [116] [Output Composability of QLoRA PEFT Modules for Plug-and-Play Attribute-Controlled Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12345)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了PEFT（参数高效微调）技术在多任务场景下的泛化方法，包括多数据集训练和两种推断时的模块组合策略，发现在推断时组合PEFT模块输出是性能最佳的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PEFT技术虽然能以较低成本进行任务特定微调，但每个新任务都需要单独微调，缺乏泛化能力。本研究旨在探索如何将PEFT技术推广到多任务组合场景，提高其在多任务环境下的实用性和效率。

Method: 提出了三种泛化方法：（1）在多个相关数据集上进行组合训练；（2）在推断时组合分别训练的PEFT模块的权重矩阵；（3）在推断时组合分别训练的PEFT模块的输出。使用三种不同的大语言模型（LLMs）、QLoRA作为PEFT技术，并在三个受控文本生成数据集（情感控制、主题控制、多属性控制）上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现求和PEFT模块输出是最强的组合方法，始终优于或等同于替代方法的性能。即使在单任务测试集上与专门单任务模块比较时，三个模块输出组合在情感控制任务上实现了所有模型平均2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: PEFT模块输出组合是一种有效的多任务泛化策略，特别是在推断阶段通过组合模块输出可以显著提升性能，为PEFT技术在多任务应用中的实际部署提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) techniques offer task-specific fine-tuning at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but require separate fine-tuning for every new task (combination). In this paper, we explore three ways of generalising beyond single-task training/inference: (i) training on combinations of multiple, related datasets; (ii) at inference, composing the weight matrices of separately trained PEFT modules; and (iii) at inference, composing the outputs of separately trained PEFT modules. We test these approaches on three different LLMs, QLoRA as the PEFT technique, and three sets of controlled text generation datasets for sentiment control, topic control, and multi-attribute control. We find that summing PEFT module outputs is a particularly strong composition method, which consistently either outperforms or matches the performance of alternative approaches. This is the case even when comparing against single-task specialised modules on the single-task test set, where three-module output composition achieves an average 2% point performance increase across all models for sentiment control.

</details>


### [117] [MedHopQA: A Disease-Centered Multi-Hop Reasoning Benchmark and Evaluation Framework for LLM-Based Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12361)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Robert Leaman,Joey Chan,Nicholas Wan,Qiao Jin,Natalie Xie,John Wilbur,Shubo Tian,Lana Yeganova,Po-Ting Lai,Chih-Hsuan Wei,Yifan Yang,Yao Ge,Qingqing Zhu,Zhizheng Wang,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedHopQA是一个疾病中心的多跳推理基准测试，包含1000个专家策划的问答对，需要整合两个维基百科文章的信息进行开放文本回答，旨在评估生物医学领域大型语言模型的推理能力而非模式匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物医学QA基准存在局限性：多项选择题允许通过答案排除而非推理来成功；广泛流传的考试风格数据集面临性能饱和和训练数据污染的风险；临床相关任务（如诊断支持、文献发现）需要的多跳推理在当前基准中代表性不足。

Method: 构建了1000个专家策划的问答对，每个问题需要综合两个不同维基百科文章的信息；采用开放文本回答格式；通过人类标注、分类、迭代验证和LLM-as-a-judge验证的结构化流程；使用MONDO、NCBI Gene和NCBI Taxonomy的本体论同义词集增强金标注；将1000个评分问题嵌入公开下载的10,000个问题集中以降低排行榜游戏和数据污染风险。

Result: MedHopQA被引入作为BioCreative IX的共享任务；提供了支持词汇和概念级别评估的基准；创建了可重复使用的框架用于构建未来生物医学QA数据集，优先考虑组合推理、抗饱和性和抗污染性作为核心设计约束。

Conclusion: MedHopQA填补了生物医学QA基准的空白，专注于多跳推理评估，能够区分推理能力和模式匹配，并提供对模型能力提升保持区分度的基准，同时降低了数据污染和排行榜游戏的风险。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in the biomedical domain requires benchmarks that can distinguish reasoning from pattern matching and remain discriminative as model capabilities improve. Existing biomedical question answering (QA) benchmarks are limited in this respect. Multiple-choice formats can allow models to succeed through answer elimination rather than inference, while widely circulated exam-style datasets are increasingly vulnerable to performance saturation and training data contamination. Multi-hop reasoning, defined as the ability to integrate information across multiple sources to derive an answer, is central to clinically meaningful tasks such as diagnostic support, literature-based discovery, and hypothesis generation, yet remains underrepresented in current biomedical QA benchmarks. MedHopQA is a disease-centered multi-hop reasoning benchmark consisting of 1,000 expert-curated question-answer pairs introduced as a shared task at BioCreative IX. Each question requires synthesis of information across two distinct Wikipedia articles, and answers are provided in an open-ended free-text format. Gold annotations are augmented with ontology-grounded synonym sets from MONDO, NCBI Gene, and NCBI Taxonomy to support both lexical and concept-level evaluation. MedHopQA was constructed through a structured process combining human annotation, triage, iterative verification, and LLM-as-a-judge validation. To reduce leaderboard gaming and contamination risk, the 1,000 scored questions are embedded within a publicly downloadable set of 10,000 questions, with answers withheld, on a CodaBench leaderboard. MedHopQA provides both a benchmark and a reusable framework for constructing future biomedical QA datasets that prioritize compositional reasoning, saturation resistance, and contamination resistance as core design constraints.

</details>


### [118] [Context Convergence Improves Answering Inferential Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12370)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在推理性问题上的表现，发现基于收敛性构建的文本段落能显著提升答案准确性，优于余弦相似度方法，表明收敛性是指导文本构建和分析LLM推理行为的有效信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型在开放域问答中广泛应用，但它们在处理推理性问题方面的能力尚未得到充分探索。这些问题的答案需要推导而非直接检索获得。研究旨在探索文本段落的结构和质量如何影响LLM在此类问题上的表现。

Method: 采用收敛性作为构建文本段落的标准，收敛性衡量句子如何有效消除错误答案。使用TriviaHG数据集的子集，将具有不同收敛水平的句子组合成文本段落。评估了六种不同规模和架构的LLM，比较了基于收敛性构建的段落与基于余弦相似度选择的段落的性能差异。

Result: 基于高收敛性句子构建的文本段落比基于余弦相似度选择的段落带来显著更好的答案准确性，表明收敛性捕捉了推理所需的有意义相关性。此外，按降序排列句子略微提高了性能，表明LLM倾向于优先处理早期信息丰富的线索。

Conclusion: 收敛性可作为构建文本段落和分析LLM推理行为的有用信号。研究突出了文本结构对LLM推理能力的重要性，并为优化推理任务的文本构建提供了实用指导。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used in open-domain Question Answering (QA), their ability to handle inferential questions-where answers must be derived rather than directly retrieved-remains still underexplored. This study investigates how the structure and quality of passages influence LLM performance on such questions. We focus on convergence, a measure of how effectively sentences (hints) eliminate incorrect answers, as a criterion for constructing passages. Using subsets of the TriviaHG dataset, we form passages by combining sentences with varying convergence levels and evaluate six LLMs of different sizes and architectures. Our results show that passages built from higher convergence sentences lead to substantially better answer accuracy than those selected by cosine similarity, indicating that convergence captures meaningful relevance for inferential reasoning. Additionally, ordering sentences by descending convergence slightly improves performance, suggesting that LLMs tend to prioritize earlier, information-rich cues. These findings highlight convergence as a practical signal for guiding passage construction and analyzing inferential reasoning behavior in LLMs.

</details>


### [119] [Scalable Token-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12384)
*Rui Min,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Minhao Cheng,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenHD是一个用于训练token级别幻觉检测器的完整流程，能够直接处理自由文本识别LLM中的幻觉，无需预先步骤分割或文本重构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理密集型任务中仍经常产生幻觉，这些幻觉难以检测，因为内容看似连贯但包含逻辑缺陷和不可靠中间结果。现有的步骤级别分析方法存在粒度有限和可扩展性差的问题

Method: 提出TokenHD管道，包含用于合成大规模幻觉注释的可扩展数据引擎，以及采用重要性加权策略的鲁棒训练方案。还提供系统的评估协议

Result: 训练后的检测器（小至0.6B）在性能上显著超越更大的推理模型（如QwQ-32B），检测性能在0.6B到8B范围内随模型规模一致扩展，且能很好地泛化到各种实际场景

Conclusion: TokenHD提供了一个有效的token级别幻觉检测框架，解决了现有方法在粒度和可扩展性上的局限性，展示了检测器规模可扩展性和跨域泛化能力，为LLM幻觉检测提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but they still frequently produce hallucinations. These hallucinations are difficult to detect in reasoning-intensive tasks, where the content appears coherent but contains errors like logical flaws and unreliable intermediate results. While step-level analysis is commonly used to detect internal hallucinations, it suffers from limited granularity and poor scalability due to its reliance on step segmentation. To address these limitations, we propose TokenHD, a holistic pipeline for training token-level hallucination detectors. Specifically, TokenHD consists of a scalable data engine for synthesizing large-scale hallucination annotations along with a training recipe featuring an importance-weighted strategy for robust model training. To systematically assess the detection performance, we also provide a rigorous evaluation protocol. Through training within TokenHD, our detector operates directly on free-form text to identify hallucinations, eliminating the need for predefined step segmentation or additional text reformatting. Our experiments show that even a small detector (0.6B) achieves substantial performance gains after training, surpassing much larger reasoning models (e.g., QwQ-32B), and detection performance scales consistently with model size from 0.6B to 8B. Finally, we show that our detector can generalize well across diverse practical scenarios and explore strategies to further enhance its cross-domain generalization capability.

</details>


### [120] [A Comparative Study of Controlled Text Generation Systems Using Level-Playing-Field Evaluation Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12395)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"公平竞争环境"（LPF）评估方法，用于对不同的可控文本生成系统进行标准化、可复现的对比评估，结果表明当前系统在标准化评估下的表现远低于原始报告水平，凸显了标准化评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可控文本生成领域存在评估不一致的问题：不同研究使用不同的数据集和评估方法，导致难以准确比较各种方法的真实性能，无法获得清晰的性能对比图景。这妨碍了该领域的健康发展和技术进步。

Method: 采用了"公平竞争环境"（LPF）评估框架：1）对系统输出进行标准化生成和处理；2）应用一套基于当前使用情况选择的共享评估方法和数据集。通过对代表性CTG系统进行重新评估，确保评估的公平性和可比性。

Result: 在LPF标准化评估下，代表性CTG系统的性能结果与原始报告结果存在显著差异，大多数系统的表现更差。这表明原始研究中的性能声明可能严重高估了系统真实能力，标准化评估揭示的性能差距比预期更大。

Conclusion: LPF评估揭示的差异表明CTG领域迫切需要标准化、可复现的评估实践。在没有统一评估标准的情况下，已发表的性能声明可能严重误导对系统真实能力的判断。标准化评估是确保该领域健康发展的关键。

Abstract: Background: Many different approaches to controlled text generation (CTG) have been proposed over recent years, but it is difficult to get a clear picture of which approach performs best, because different datasets and evaluation methods are used in each case to assess the control achieved.
  Objectives: Our aim in the work reported in this paper is to develop an approach to evaluation that enables us to comparatively evaluate different CTG systems in a manner that is both informative and fair to the individual systems.
  Methods: We use a level-playing-field (LPF) approach to comparative evaluation where we (i) generate and process all system outputs in a standardised way, and (ii) apply a shared set of evaluation methods and datasets, selected based on those currently in use, in order to ensure fair evaluation.
  Results: When re-evaluated in this way, performance results for a representative set of current CTG systems differ substantially from originally reported results, in most cases for the worse. This highlights the importance of a shared standardised way of assessing controlled generation.
  Conclusions: The discrepancies revealed by LPF evaluation demonstrate the urgent need for standardised, reproducible evaluation practices in CTG. Our results suggest that without such practices, published performance claims may substantially misrepresent true system capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个级联商品展示框架，通过生成式AI将电商主页构建分解为两个生成任务：页面板块主题生成和约束关键词生成，以替代传统独立组件模式，提升了页面个性化和语义连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商大型市场的个性化主页通常由多个独立组件组装而成，包括每个页面板块的静态主题、每个板块的检索系统获取合格产品，以及逐点排序器排列内容。虽然这种模式在优化总体偏好方面有效，但其僵化性限制了页面间的个性化和语义连贯性，难以支持随时间变化的动态目标和商品展示需求。

Method: 引入了级联商品展示框架，将主页构建分解为两个生成式任务：(1) 板块级别的主题生成；(2) 每个板块的约束关键词生成以驱动产品检索。利用师生微调来提高该框架在生产延迟和成本约束下的可扩展性。通过微调模型消融实验接近闭源大语言模型的性能。进一步贡献了AI驱动的内容评估和质量过滤框架，实现动态内容的安全自动化大规模部署。生成式输出与传统排序模型融合以保留混合基础设施。

Result: 在线实验表明，该框架在每次页面浏览的购物车添加量上实现了约+2.7%的提升（相对于强基线）。微调模型消融实验显示能接近闭源大语言模型的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的级联生成式商品展示框架通过将主页构建分解为生成式任务，解决了传统独立组件模式的僵化问题，显著提升了页面个性化和语义连贯性，同时通过师生微调、AI质量评估和混合融合等方法确保了生产环境的可扩展性和安全性，最终实现了显著的在线性能提升。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [122] [EVOCHAMBER: Test-Time Co-evolution of Multi-Agent System at Individual, Team, and Population Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11136)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Shengyu Dai,Zhenwen Shao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOCHAMBER是一个无需训练的多智能体测试时演化框架，通过不对称知识路由和三层演化机制（核心CODREAM协议、团队级操作、种群级操作），使同质初始化的智能体自发演化出稳定的专业化分工，在异构任务流上实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时方法存在局限性：单智能体方法只能演化自身上下文和记忆，无法支持多智能体间的协作演化；传统多智能体方法要么将经验局限于个体智能体（放弃跨智能体学习），要么对称广播给所有智能体（消除专业化价值）。真正的多智能体演化应能演化"谁协作"、"如何协作"以及"知识如何在种群中流动"等单智能体不具备的能力。

Method: EVOCHAMBER框架包含三个层次的演化：1）核心CODREAM协议：在任务失败或分歧时触发协作式反思，从强智能体向弱智能体进行不对称知识路由，填补知识缺口同时保留专业化；2）团队级操作：在线组装面向特定领域的团队并选择协作结构；3）种群级生命周期操作：在性能压力下对智能体进行分叉、合并、修剪和播种操作。整个框架使用Qwen3-8B作为基础模型。

Result: 在三个异构任务流上的实验结果显示：竞争数学任务达到63.9%，代码任务达到75.7%，多领域推理达到87.1%。在数学任务上相对最佳基线提升32%。从同质初始化的智能体出发，自发演化出4-5个稳定的领域专家。消融实验确认不对称跨智能体传递是性能提升的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 多智能体测试时演化不仅仅是单智能体演化的重复，而是能产生单智能体系统无法实现的结构特征（如自发专业化分工）。EVOCHAMBER框架通过三层演化机制和不对称知识路由，实现了真正的多智能体演化，为测试时自适应系统提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We argue that multi-agent test-time evolution is not single-agent evolution replicated N times. A single-agent learner can only evolve its own context and memory. A multi-agent system additionally evolves who collaborates, how they collaborate, and how knowledge flows across the population. These components have no single-agent counterpart and can produce phenomena such as emergent specialization. Yet prior test-time methods either confine experiences to individual agents, forfeiting cross-agent learning, or broadcast symmetrically to all agents, erasing the specialization that makes collaboration valuable. We present EVOCHAMBER, a training-free framework that instantiates test-time evolution at three levels over a coevolving agent pool. At its core is CODREAM (Collaborative Dreaming), a post-task protocol triggered on team failure or disagreement, in which agents collaboratively reflect, distill insights, and route them asymmetrically from strong to weak agents on the failed niche, preserving specialization while filling knowledge gaps. Team-level operators assemble niche-conditioned teams and select collaboration structures online. Population-level lifecycle operators fork, merge, prune, and seed agents under performance pressure. On three heterogeneous task streams with Qwen3-8B, EVOCHAMBER reaches 63.9% on competition math, 75.7% on code, and 87.1% on multi-domain reasoning, outperforming the best baseline by 32% relative on math and confirming asymmetric cross-agent transfer as the primary driver in ablation. Starting from several identically initialized agents, four to five stable niche specialists spontaneously emerge, a structural signature of multi-agent evolution that no single-agent learner can express. See our code at: https://github.com/Mercury7353/EvoChamber

</details>


### [123] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankQ：一种离线到在线强化学习方法，通过自监督多项目排序损失来增强时序差分学习，学习相对动作偏好而非统一惩罚未见动作，从而塑造Q函数引导策略向更优行为改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线到在线RL方法通过悲观主义处理价值高估问题，通常对分布外动作进行降权处理。但这种方法本质上充当了行为克隆锚点，当数据集动作次优时会阻碍下游在线策略改进。需要一种更灵活的方法来学习动作的相对偏好而非简单惩罚未见动作。

Method: 提出RankQ方法，将时序差分学习与自监督多项目排序损失相结合。该方法不是统一惩罚分布外动作，而是学习动作的相对偏好排序，从而塑造Q函数使得动作梯度指向更高质量的行为。通过这种方式，即使在数据覆盖有限的场景下也能学习准确的评价函数。

Result: 在稀疏奖励D4RL基准测试中，RankQ在性能上与7种先前方法竞争或优于它们。在基于视觉的机器人学习中：1）低数据场景下，比次优方法平均提高42.7%的模拟成功率；2）高数据场景下，比次优方法提高13.7%的模拟性能；3）在模拟到真实迁移中，将真实世界立方体堆叠成功率从VLA初始性能的43.1%提升到84.7%。

Conclusion: RankQ通过引入排序损失学习相对动作偏好，相比传统的悲观主义方法能更有效地引导策略改进，在离线到在线RL中实现了显著性能提升，特别在视觉语言动作模型的微调中表现出色，具有很强的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [124] [OLIVIA: Online Learning via Inference-time Action Adaptation for Decision Making in LLM ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11169)
*Sheldon Yu,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OLIVIA：一个推理时动作适配框架，通过将LLM的最终动作选择层建模为上下文线性赌博机，为ReAct风格智能体提供显式决策层，支持在线更新和不确定性估计，从而在部署时高效改进任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署环境中，LLM智能体重复处理相关的多步任务时，小的动作选择错误会累积导致工具调用浪费、延迟增加和可靠性降低。现有的推理时适配方法主要依赖提示或检索，通过上下文操纵间接影响行为，缺乏显式决策层来评分候选动作、表示不确定性或从动作级反馈进行在线更新。

Method: OLIVIA将ReAct风格智能体的最终动作选择层建模为候选动作上的上下文线性赌博机，使用冻结的隐藏状态作为决策上下文。采用置信上界探索策略，通过动作级反馈进行轻量级在线更新，在保持底层推理过程不变的同时直接适配动作选择行为。

Result: 在四个基准测试中实例化OLIVIA，实验表明它始终优于静态ReAct和基于提示的推理时基线方法。该方法能够以最小的计算开销实现样本高效的策略改进。

Conclusion: 显式的在线决策层为LLM智能体部署时的适配提供了有效的替代方案，优于纯粹基于提示或检索的方法。OLIVIA框架通过直接适配动作选择接口、保持推理过程完整性、提供显式不确定性估计和轻量级在线更新，实现了部署时行为的有效改进。

Abstract: Large language model agents interleave reasoning, action selection, and observation to solve sequential decision-making tasks. In deployed settings where agents repeatedly handle related multi-step tasks, small action-selection errors can accumulate into wasted tool calls, latency, and reduced reliability. Despite this need for deployment-time improvement, existing inference-time adaptation methods for LLM agents mainly rely on prompting or retrieval, which influence behavior indirectly through context manipulation. For ReAct-style agents, such approaches do not expose an explicit decision layer that can score candidate actions, represent uncertainty, or be updated online from action-level feedback. As a result, they provide limited support for trackable, fine-grained, and uncertainty-aware adaptation during deployment. We propose OLIVIA, an inference-time action adaptation framework for ReAct-style agents. OLIVIA models the LLM's final action-selection layer as a contextual linear bandit over candidate actions, with frozen hidden states as decision contexts. This choice is particularly suitable for deployment because it adapts behavior directly at the action-selection interface, preserves the underlying reasoning process, and provides explicit uncertainty estimates and lightweight online updates from action-level feedback. With upper-confidence-bound exploration, OLIVIA improves the policy sample-efficiently with minimal computational overhead. We instantiate OLIVIA on four benchmarks and show that it consistently improves task performance over static ReAct and prompt-based inference-time baselines. Our results suggest that explicit online decision layers provide an effective alternative to purely prompt- or retrieval-based adaptation for LLM agents during deployment.

</details>


### [125] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了策略蒸馏(OPD)和策略自蒸馏(OPSD)在大型语言模型中的有效性，发现其成功与否高度依赖于具体设置：OPD在数学推理中对教师选择和损失公式敏感；OPSD在实例特定特权信息缺失时失败，但在共享潜在规则下有效。论文识别了三种失败机制并提出了相应缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略蒸馏(OPD)和策略自蒸馏(OPSD)作为大型语言模型的后训练方法表现出潜力，但现有研究结果不一致：一方面在系统提示和知识内化方面有效，另一方面也存在不稳定性和性能下降的问题。需要系统理解这些方法何时有效、何时失败及其原因。

Method: 进行了全面的实证研究：1）分析OPD在数学推理任务中对教师选择和损失公式的敏感性；2）研究OPSD在不同特权信息(PI)设置下的表现；3）识别三种失败机制：教师-学生分布不匹配、优化不稳定性、OPSD特定限制；4）提出缓解方法：停止梯度TopK目标、RLVR适应教师、SFT稳定学生。

Result: 研究发现：1）OPD在数学推理中高度依赖教师选择和损失公式；2）OPSD在测试时缺少实例特定特权信息(PI)时会失败，但在PI代表共享潜在规则时有效；3）识别了三种失败机制：分布不匹配、优化不稳定、OPSD特定限制；4）提出的缓解方法能有效减轻这些失败。

Conclusion: OPD和OPSD的有效性高度依赖于具体应用场景和设置。理解失败机制并采用适当的技术改进可以显著提高这些方法的稳定性。当特权信息代表共享规则时，OPSD是有效的；但当需要实例特定信息时，OPSD面临根本性挑战。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [126] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）中的嵌入式数值锚点会系统性偏差图像质量判断，这种偏差无法简化为视觉变化，且与模型的表示动态相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究视觉语言模型中是否存在类似语言模型中的数字锚定偏差现象，特别是嵌入在图像中的数字锚点如何影响模型对图像质量的主观评价，以及这种偏差的认知机制和表示基础。

Method: 研究使用了六种来自五个不同架构家族的视觉语言模型。方法包括：1）行为实验：测量图像中嵌入数值锚点对质量判断的系统性影响；2）分层探测：分析不同层次表示与锚点分类和图像质量预测的关系；3）融合分析：研究不同架构中视觉和语言特征的整合模式。

Result: 主要结果包括：1）所有VLMs都表现出显著的锚定偏差（ANOVA η² = 0.18-0.77，p < 0.001）；2）锚点效应比严重图像质量退化效应大2.5倍；3）锚点分类饱和的层次（L12-L34）对质量预测不是最优的，最优层次更深（R² = 0.69-0.91）；4）不同架构的融合模式不同：两个模型在L1-L2层实现即时融合，三个模型实现部分或无融合。

Conclusion: 该研究为视觉锚定偏差提供了因果解释框架，将模型的行为易感性与其表示动态联系起来。研究证实了视觉语言模型中存在系统性的锚定偏差，这种偏差源于视觉和语言特征在不同层次的融合模式差异，且无法通过简单的视觉特征变化来解释。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [127] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLATIM是一个针对物理解谜游戏《不可思议的机器2》的新基准测试，专门评估视觉语言模型在复杂物理推理和精确鼠标交互方面的能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试忽视了点击式解谜游戏所需的复杂物理推理能力，特别是在高层逻辑推理与需要精确鼠标交互的连续动作空间之间存在重要差距

Method: 提出VLATIM基准，基于经典物理解谜游戏《不可思议的机器2》构建，包含五个渐进部分，评估从基础视觉接地和领域理解到多步操作和完整解谜的能力

Result: 结果显示推理与执行之间存在显著差异：大型专有模型表现出优越的规划能力，但在精确视觉接地方面存在困难，尚未展现出类似人类的解决问题的能力

Conclusion: VLATIM基准突出了视觉语言模型在交互环境中的局限性，特别是在连接高层推理与精确物理操作方面，为评估人类逻辑问题解决能力提供了专门工具

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [128] [PIVOT: Bridging Planning and Execution in LLM Agents via Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11225)
*Tuo Zhang,Alin-Ionut Popa,Yan Xu,Rui Song,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIVOT是一个用于缓解LLM智能体计划与执行偏差的自我监督框架，通过计划-检查-演化轨迹的迭代优化，显著提升了约束满足率并减少了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)智能体经常生成看似连贯但在执行时会失败的计划，原因包括不可行的行动、约束违反以及长时间范围中的误差累积。这些计划与执行之间的偏差需要有效解决。

Method: PIVOT采用四阶段框架：1) PLAN生成候选轨迹；2) INSPECT执行并计算结构化损失，通过文本梯度编码计划执行差异；3) EVOLVE应用这些信号产生改进轨迹；4) VERIFY执行最终全局约束检查。采用单调接受过程确保解决方案质量非递减。

Result: 在DeepPlanning和GAIA上的实验表明：人工在环(HITL)反馈下，PIVOT在约束满足方面实现了高达94%的相对改进；完全自主变体也保持了显著增益。同时，计算效率高，比竞争方法减少3-5倍的token使用。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的轨迹优化（无论是自我监督还是人工监督）是缓解自主智能体系统中计划与执行间隙的原则性方法，PIVOT框架为这一方向提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents frequently generate seemingly coherent plans that fail upon execution due to infeasible actions, constraint violations, and compounding errors over extended horizons. PIVOT (Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories) addresses this plan-execution misalignment through a self-supervised framework that treats trajectories as optimizable objects iteratively refined via environment interaction. The framework comprises four stages: PLAN generates candidate trajectories; INSPECT executes them and computes structured losses with textual gradients encoding plan-execution discrepancies; EVOLVE applies these signals to produce improved trajectories; and VERIFY performs a final global check against task constraints. A monotonic acceptance process ensures a non-decreasing solution quality. Empirical evaluations on DeepPlanning and GAIA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: with human-in-the-loop (HITL) feedback, PIVOT establishes a strong upper bound up to 94% relative improvement in constraint satisfaction, while its fully autonomous variant retains substantial gains, showing that the core trajectory-refinement mechanism remains effective without external supervision. At the same time, PIVOT remains computationally efficient, requiring up to 3x to 5x fewer tokens than competing refinement methods. These findings establish that (self- or human-supervised) feedback-based trajectory optimization is a principled methodology for mitigating plan-execution gaps in autonomous agent systems.

</details>


### [129] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入类比推理（AR）来解决AI系统在开放式问题解决中的模式崩溃问题，通过跨领域类比生成新颖多样的解决方案，并在四个生物医学问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学需要AI系统能够为开放式问题生成新颖多样的解决方案，但当前大型语言模型在解决这类问题时容易出现模式崩溃（生成低多样性结果），限制了科学发现的潜力。

Method: 提出类比推理（AR）方法：基于共享关系结构生成跨领域问题的类比，然后利用这些类比搜索新颖解决方案。该方法包括三个核心步骤：1）识别问题中的关系结构；2）寻找具有相似结构的跨领域问题；3）从类比中推导新的解决方案。

Result: AR方法显著提高了解决方案的多样性（改进90-173%），超过50%的时间生成新颖解决方案（基线仅为1.6%）。在四个生物医学问题上的验证显示：1）扰动效应预测的分布指标提升近13倍；2）细胞-细胞通信预测的AUPRC超越所有基线；3）大脑区域交互推断与已发表方法高度相关（ρ=0.729）；4）寡核苷酸性质预测在两个数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 类比推理是解决AI系统在开放式问题解决中模式崩溃的有效方法，能够生成新颖且多样化的解决方案，可用于增强现有解决方案生成方法的搜索空间，在生物医学等复杂科学领域具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [130] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentRouter是一种多模态大语言模型路由系统，通过反事实多模态效用预测来匹配图像-问题输入的需求与候选模型的能力，从而优化模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在不同任务（OCR、图表理解、空间推理、视觉问答等）上具有异质性优势。现有的路由方法往往只估计查询难度，而无法匹配输入的多模态需求与各候选模型的具体能力，导致路由效果不佳。

Method: LatentRouter将MLLM路由建模为反事实多模态效用预测问题。它从图像-问题查询中提取学习的多模态路由胶囊，用模型能力令牌表示每个候选MLLM，通过这些状态之间的潜在通信来估计每个模型如果被选择时的表现。该方法包含分布结果头来预测模型特定的反事实质量，以及有界胶囊校正来优化临界决策。

Result: 在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上的实验表明，LatentRouter优于固定模型、特征级和基于学习的路由基线。改进在多模态任务组（模型选择依赖于视觉、布局敏感或推理导向要求）上最为显著，潜在通信是改进的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: LatentRouter通过反事实多模态效用预测实现了有效的MLLM路由，支持性能导向和性能-成本路由，并能通过共享每模型评分和可用性掩码处理变化的候选模型池。该方法为匹配多模态需求与模型能力提供了有效的路由框架。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [131] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CDVM方法解决数据修剪中低数据场景下的优化问题，该方法在OpenDataVal基准测试中表现良好且运行时间具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley值的数据价值评估方法在保留数据量很少时，对低价值数据修剪的效果不理想。当数据量有限时，传统的基于Shapley值的数据价值评估方法无法优化数据集的整体影响力。

Method: 提出约束数据价值最大化（CDVM）方法，将数据修剪建模为约束优化问题：最大化数据集的总体影响力，同时惩罚对单个测试样本的过度贡献。该方法专门针对低数据场景设计。

Result: 在OpenDataVal基准测试中，CDVM表现出色且运行时间具有竞争力。特别在仅保留少量数据时，相比传统基于Shapley值的方法，CDVM能更有效地修剪低价值数据。

Conclusion: 基于Shapley值的数据价值评估方法在低数据场景下存在局限性，而CDVM通过约束优化框架解决了这一问题，为低数据场景下的数据修剪提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [132] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的RAG-based幻觉检测基准T RIVIA+，该基准包含最长的上下文样本和四种噪声标签方案，用于全面评估幻觉检测器性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测基准存在两个主要缺陷：1）缺乏具有长上下文的RAG-based基准数据集；2）没有提供现实的标签噪声来压力测试检测器。这些缺陷限制了幻觉检测器的有效评估。

Method: 首先建立了幻觉检测基准的理想属性集合，然后通过严格的人工标注流程构建了T RIVIA+基准，该基准包含长上下文样本并设计了四种不同类型的噪声标签方案。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）当前检测器在RAG-based基准上仍有很大提升空间；2）基础的LLM-as-a-Judge基线方法表现具有竞争力；3）标签噪声会显著降低检测性能。

Conclusion: T RIVIA+基准填补了现有幻觉检测基准的重要空白，为RAG-based任务中的幻觉检测研究提供了更全面、现实的评估工具，将推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [133] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个用于生成式AI因果公平性的理论框架，统一了标准机器学习与生成式AI设置，发展了新的因果分解方法以量化不同因果路径的公平性影响，并建立了识别条件和高效估计器，应用于大型语言模型的种族和性别偏见分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在高风险领域的部署日益增多，引发了关于公平性和人口统计学差异延续的严重关切。因果推理为公平性分析提供了原则性框架，但现有因果公平性研究主要关注标准机器学习设置，而生成式AI环境则更为复杂，因为生成模型可以从任意条件分布中采样，隐式构建所有因果机制的信念，而非仅学习单一预测函数。这种根本性差异需要新的因果公平性方法论发展。

Method: 研究形式化了生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，并将其与标准ML设置统一在一个共同的理论框架下。推导了新的因果分解结果，能够沿着(a)不同的因果路径和(b)生成模型机制对真实世界机制的替换这两个维度，对公平性影响进行细粒度量化。建立了感兴趣的因果量的识别条件，并引入了高效估计器。

Result: 研究展示了方法论的价值，通过分析不同数据集中大型语言模型的种族和性别偏见进行实证验证。提出的框架能够量化生成式AI系统中的公平性影响，识别偏见产生的具体因果路径，并为评估和缓解生成模型中的偏见提供了系统化方法。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的因果公平性需要不同于标准机器学习的方法论扩展。研究提供了一个统一的因果框架和新的分解技术，能够更精细地理解和量化生成式AI系统中的公平性影响，为实际应用中识别和缓解偏见提供了理论和技术基础。

Abstract: Automated systems built on artificial intelligence (AI) are increasingly deployed across high-stakes domains, raising critical concerns about fairness and the perpetuation of demographic disparities that exist in the world. In this context, causal inference provides a principled framework for reasoning about fairness, as it links observed disparities to underlying mechanisms and aligns naturally with human intuition and legal notions of discrimination. Prior work on causal fairness primarily focuses on the standard machine learning setting, where a decision-maker constructs a single predictive mechanism $f_{\widehat Y}$ for an outcome variable $Y$, while inheriting the causal mechanisms of all other covariates from the real world. The generative AI setting, however, is markedly more complex: generative models can sample from arbitrary conditionals over any set of variables, implicitly constructing their own beliefs about all causal mechanisms rather than learning a single predictive function. This fundamental difference requires new developments in causal fairness methodology. We formalize the problem of causal fairness in generative AI and unify it with the standard ML setting under a common theoretical framework. We then derive new causal decomposition results that enable granular quantification of fairness impacts along both (a) different causal pathways and (b) the replacement of real-world mechanisms by the generative model's mechanisms. We establish identification conditions and introduce efficient estimators for causal quantities of interest, and demonstrate the value of our methodology by analyzing race and gender bias in large language models across different datasets.

</details>


### [134] [Causal Algorithmic Recourse: Foundations and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11373)
*Drago Plecko,Collin Wang,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的因果框架，用于建模算法可回溯性（algorithmic recourse）过程，考虑了预干预和后干预结果的稳定性以及潜在变量的重采样。该方法引入了后回溯稳定性条件，允许仅从观察数据推断回溯效应，并开发了基于copula的算法和分布自由算法来处理不同类型的回溯数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可回溯性方法通常将回溯结果视为固定单元的反事实，忽略了现实世界中回溯涉及同一主体在不同潜在条件下的重复决策过程。这导致了对算法回溯真实效果的错误理解，因为真实世界中的回溯是一个动态过程，涉及主体的多次决策，且潜在变量可能发生变化。

Method: 1. 开发了一个因果框架，将回溯建模为预干预和后干预结果的过程，允许部分稳定性和潜在变量的重采样
2. 引入后回溯稳定性条件，使得仅从观察数据就能推理回溯
3. 开发了基于copula的算法来推断在这些条件下的回溯效应
4. 对于有配对观察数据（回溯数据）的情况，开发了推断copula参数和拟合优度检验的方法
5. 当copula模型被拒绝时，提供了直接从回溯数据学习回溯效应的分布自由算法

Result: 1. 提出了一套完整的理论框架和算法来处理现实世界中的算法可回溯性问题
2. 在真实和半合成数据集上证明了所提方法的价值
3. 能够处理两种数据情况：仅有观察数据的情况和有配对回溯数据的情况
4. 提供了从简单参数化模型到完全非参数方法的完整解决方案

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个更符合现实世界的算法可回溯性框架，克服了传统方法将回溯视为固定单元反事实的局限性。通过考虑回溯过程的动态性和潜在变量的变化，该方法能够更准确地推理和计算真实世界中的回溯效应，为构建更可信的AI决策系统提供了重要的理论和方法基础。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of AI decision-making systems is increasingly important. A key feature of such systems is the ability to provide recommendations for how an individual may reverse a negative decision, a problem known as algorithmic recourse. Existing approaches treat recourse outcomes as counterfactuals of a fixed unit, ignoring that real-world recourse involves repeated decisions on the same individual under possibly different latent conditions. We develop a causal framework that models recourse as a process over pre- and post-intervention outcomes, allowing for partial stability and resampling of latent variables. We introduce post-recourse stability conditions that enable reasoning about recourse from observational data alone, and develop a copula-based algorithm for inferring the effects of recourse under these conditions. For settings where paired observations of the same individual before and after intervention are available (called recourse data), we develop methods for inferring copula parameters and performing goodness-of-fit testing. When the copula model is rejected, we provide a distribution-free algorithm for learning recourse effects directly from recourse data. We demonstrate the value of the proposed methods on real and semi-synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [135] [LLM-X: A Scalable Negotiation-Oriented Exchange for Communication Among Personal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11376)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LLM-X：一个用于个人LLM之间可扩展的、面向协商的交换环境，支持结构化的多智能体直接通信，具有模式有效性和策略执行保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具为中心的协议主要关注智能体-API交互，缺乏对LLM之间直接协调的有效支持，特别是在大规模多智能体协商场景中。

Method: 设计了包含联合网关、基于主题的路由和策略执行的架构；开发了支持能力协商和合同网式协调的类型化消息协议；进行了大规模LLM多智能体协商的首次实证评估。

Result: 在5、9、12个智能体规模下，使用不同协商策略（低、中、高），进行短时（分钟级）和长时（2小时、12小时）负载测试。结果显示明确的策略-性能权衡：更严格的策略提高了鲁棒性和公平性，但增加了延迟和消息量。长时间运行证实LLM-X在持续负载下保持稳定，延迟漂移有界。

Conclusion: LLM-X为LLM之间的直接协调提供了可扩展的解决方案，证明了在大规模多智能体协商中的可行性和稳定性，揭示了策略选择在性能与鲁棒性之间的重要权衡。

Abstract: We propose a personal-LLM exchange (LLM-X), a scalable negotiation-oriented environment that enables direct, structured communication across populations of personal agents (LLMs), each representing an individual user. Unlike existing tool-centric protocols that focus on agent-API interaction, LLM-X introduces a message bus and routing substrate for LLM-to-LLM coordination with guarantees around schema validity and policy enforcement. We contribute: (1) an architecture for LLM-X comprising federated gateways, topic-based routing, and policy enforcement; (2) a typed message protocol supporting capability negotiation and contract-net-style coordination; and (3) the first empirical evaluation of LLM-based multi-agent negotiation at scale. Experiments span 5, 9, and 12 agents, under distinct negotiation policies (Low, Medium, High), and across both short-run (minutes) and long-run (2h, 12h) load conditions. Results highlight clear policy-performance trade-offs: stricter policies improve robustness and fairness but increase latencies and message volume. Extended runs confirm that LLM-X remains stable under sustained load, with bounded latency drift.

</details>


### [136] [Revisiting Privacy Preservation in Brain-Computer Interfaces: Conceptual Boundaries, Risk Pathways, and a Protection-Strength Grading Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11386)
*Lei Sun,Xiuqing Mao,Shuai Zhang,Qingyu Zeng,Min Zhao,Jiyuan Li,Wenle Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文回顾了脑机接口(BCI)隐私保护研究，提出了一个三维框架来分类现有工作的保护强度等级，并讨论了神经伦理风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着BCI从实验室研究快速进入临床、边缘和现实世界环境，神经数据、衍生表示、模型资产和解码输出可能被重新关联到个体，或在采集、传输、存储、训练、推理和反馈过程中用于推断超出任务要求的信息，存在显著的隐私风险扩展

Method: 从通用BCI范式出发，定义了隐私保护边界、保护对象以及用户数据隐私与模型隐私在共享风险路径中的关系。提出了一个三维框架：保护对象、生命周期阶段和主导保护强度水平，将现有工作分类为四个保护强度等级

Result: 开发了一个系统化的分类框架，用于评估BCI隐私保护方法的保护强度，该框架能够将现有研究成果组织到不同的保护等级中，从低级保护到高级保护

Conclusion: BCI隐私保护不仅需要模糊数据，还需要在保留下游效用的同时分离与任务无关的敏感信息。将心理隐私和神经伦理风险视为开放性问题，强调需要全面的隐私保护方法

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) are moving rapidly from laboratory research into clinical, edge, and real-world settings. Under ISO/IEC 8663:2025, a BCI is a direct communication link between central nervous system activity and external software or hardware systems. This link expands privacy risk beyond raw neural-signal leakage: neural data, derived representations, model assets, and decoded outputs can be re-associated with individuals across collection, transmission, storage, training, inference, and feedback, or used to infer information beyond what a task requires. Starting from the general BCI paradigm, this review deffnes privacy-protection boundaries, protection objects, and the relationship between user data privacy and model privacy within a shared risk pathway. It then proposes a three-dimensional framework - protection object, lifecycle stage, and dominant protection-strength level - to classify existing work into four levels of protection strength. Finally, mental privacy and neuroethical risks are treated as open issues, emphasizing that BCI privacy protection should not only obscure data but also disentangle task-irrelevant sensitive information while preserving downstream utility. Keywords: Brain-computer interface, Neural data privacy, User data privacy, Model privacy, Disentanglement of task-irrelevant sensitive information, Protection-strength grading, Neuroethical risks

</details>


### [137] [Transformer Interpretability from Perspective of Attention and Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11392)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过引导梯度/注意力方向实现Transformer解释的方法，利用ViT与人类感知差异进行几乎不可察觉的图像类别修改，揭示潜在安全风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然研究者更关注Transformer模型的性能，但Transformer的解释性不可忽视。梯度被广泛用于Transformer解释，需要从注意力和梯度角度深入研究Transformer的解释机制。

Method: 从注意力和梯度角度研究Transformer解释，提出通过引导梯度方向（更准确说是注意力方向）的方法来实现Transformer解释。具体利用Vision Transformer（ViT）和人类感知图像的差异，通过细微改变图像来修改其分类结果。

Result: 该方法能够更全面地解释特征区域，提供细节解释，帮助更好理解Transformer机制。通过几乎人眼不可察觉的方式改变图像，可以实现类别重写，这在某些场景下可能带来安全风险。

Conclusion: 提出的基于梯度/注意力方向引导的Transformer解释方法不仅能提高模型可解释性，还揭示了ViT与人类感知差异带来的类别重写安全风险，强调了在关注模型性能的同时必须重视解释性和安全性问题。

Abstract: Although researchers' attention is more focused on the performance of Transformer models, the interpretation of Transformer can never be ignored. Gradient is widely utilized in Transformer interpretation. From the perspective of attention and gradient, we conduct an in-depth study of Transformer interpretation and propose a method to achieve it by guiding the gradient direction, or more precisely, the attention direction. The method enables more comprehensive interpretation of feature regions, offers detail interpretation, and helps to better understand Transformer mechanism. Leveraging the difference in how Vision Transformer (ViT) and humans perceive images, we alter the class of an image in a way that is almost imperceptible to the human eye. This class rewriting phenomenon may potentially pose security risks in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [138] [AcuityBench: Evaluating Clinical Acuity Identification and Uncertainty Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11398)
*Robin Linzmayer,Georgianna Lin,Di Coneybeare,Jason Chu,Trudi Cloyd,Manish Garg,Miles Gordon,Elizabeth Hartofilis,Benjamin Hong,Ashraf Hussain,Eugene Y. Kim,Oluchi Iheagwara King,Ross McCormack,Erica Olsen,John K. Riggins,Mustafa N. Rasheed,Dana L. Sacco,Vinay Saggar,Osman R. Sayan,Amit Shembekar,Janice Shin-Kim,Wendy W. Sun,Bernard P. Chang,David Kessler,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcuityBench是一个评估语言模型从用户医疗描述中识别适当护理急迫性的基准，整合了五个公共数据集，包含914个案例，支持QA分类和自由对话两种评估格式，揭示了模型在护理急迫性判断上的系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康基准主要关注医疗问答、广泛的健康交互或特定工作流的分诊任务，但缺乏在统一框架下评估护理急迫性识别能力。护理急迫性判断是医疗AI应用中的关键安全能力，需要专门基准来系统评估模型在实际健康应用场景中的表现。

Method: 通过协调五个公共数据集（用户对话、在线论坛帖子、临床小案例、患者门户消息），建立共享的四级护理急迫性框架（从家庭监测到立即急诊）。包含914个案例，其中697个共识案例用于标准准确率评估，217个医生确认的模糊案例用于不确定性感知评估。支持两种任务格式：QA设置中的显式四分类，以及基于相同框架的基于量规的对话响应评估。

Result: 在12个前沿专有和开源模型中发现，在明确案例的护理急迫性准确率存在显著差异和错误方向。任务格式比较显示系统权衡：对话响应相对于QA减少了过度分诊但增加了分诊不足，特别是在高急迫性案例中。在模糊案例中，没有模型能接近匹配医生判断分布，模型预测比专家临床不确定性更集中。专家和模型裁决的比较显示，临床不确定性在标签分歧中起重要作用。

Conclusion: 护理急迫性识别是一项独特的安全关键能力，AcuityBench能够系统比较和压力测试模型在现实世界健康应用中如何引导用户到适当护理水平。基准为评估语言模型在医疗分诊场景中的表现提供了标准化框架，突出了模型与临床专家在不确定情况下的决策差异。

Abstract: We introduce AcuityBench, a benchmark for evaluating whether language models identify the appropriate urgency of care from user medical presentations. Existing health benchmarks emphasize medical question answering, broad health interactions, or narrow workflow-specific triage tasks, but they do not offer a unified evaluation of acuity identification across these settings. AcuityBench addresses this gap by harmonizing five public datasets spanning user conversations, online forum posts, clinical vignettes, and patient portal messages under a shared four-level acuity framework ranging from home monitoring to immediate emergency care. The benchmark contains 914 cases, including 697 consensus cases for standard accuracy evaluation and 217 physician-confirmed ambiguous cases for uncertainty-aware evaluation. It supports two complementary task formats: explicit four-way classification in a QA setting, and free-form conversational responses evaluated with a rubric-based judge anchored to the same framework. Across 12 frontier proprietary and open-weight models, we find substantial variation in clear-case acuity accuracy and error direction. Comparing task formats reveals a systematic tradeoff: conversational responses reduce over-triage but increase under-triage relative to QA, especially in higher-acuity cases. In ambiguous cases, no model closely matches the distribution of physician judgments, and model predictions are more concentrated than expert clinical uncertainty. We also compare expert and model adjudication on a subset of maximally ambiguous cases, using those cases to examine the role of clinical uncertainty in label disagreement. Together, these results position acuity identification as a distinct safety-critical capability and show that AcuityBench enables systematic comparison and stress-testing of how well models guide users to the right level of care in real-world health use.

</details>


### [139] [Attributing Emergence in Million-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11404)
*Ling Tang,Jilin Mei,Qian Chen,Qihan Ren,Linfeng Zhang,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Aumann-Shapley路径积分归因方法适配到大语言模型驱动的百万规模多智能体系统的方法，解决了现有方法规模限制问题，证明了非线性宏观指标下小规模样本归因与全规模归因之间存在不可调和的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于公理的归因方法在N<10^3规模时就会出现组合爆炸，而实际社会现象（如极化、信息级联、市场恐慌）发生在N≥10^6的规模。现有小规模研究与全规模研究之间存在规模差距，小规模样本存在可见性偏差，导致归因结果失真。

Method: 将Aumann-Shapley路径积分归因方法适配到LLM驱动的多智能体系统，使其能在百万智能体规模下运行。该方法满足所有四个公理，相比相同硬件上的采样Shapley方法，运行速度快4-5个数量级。

Result: 在14天的Bluesky公开数据（1,671,587活跃用户）上进行实证测试：全规模归因显示长尾和中层用户共同承担多数影响，而有偏差的小规模样本（N=10^2）则几乎将所有影响归因于少数高关注度账户，二者存在结构性差异。

Conclusion: 提出了"归因缩放偏差定理"，证明在任何非线性宏观指标下，小规模归因与全规模归因之间的差异无法通过后验缩放因子调和。全规模归因不是方法选择问题，而是任何非线性宏观指标的理论要求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can simulate human-like reasoning and decision-making in individual agents. LLM-powered multi-agent systems (MAS) combine such agents to simulate population-scale social phenomena such as polarization, information cascades, and market panics. Such studies require attributing macro emergence to individual agents, but existing axiomatic methods scale combinatorially in $N$ and have been confined to $N \lesssim 10^3$, while the phenomena they explain occur at $N \geq 10^6$. We address this gap by adapting Aumann--Shapley path-integral attribution to LLM-powered MAS at million-agent scale; the resulting method satisfies all four axioms, runs four to five orders of magnitude faster than sampled Shapley on the same hardware. We use this method to test the scale gap empirically: across 14 days of public Bluesky data ($1{,}671{,}587$ active users), we compute the attribution at both full scale and the visibility-biased $N = 10^2$ convenience sample used by small-scale studies, and the two disagree structurally. At full scale the long tail and middle tier jointly carry the majority; the biased small panel attributes almost everything to a few high-follower accounts. We then prove that under any nonlinear macro indicator the disagreement cannot be reduced by post-hoc rescaling: an Attribution Scaling Bias theorem shows that no global rescaling factor can reconcile small-scale and full-scale attribution. Full-scale attribution is therefore not a methodological choice but a theoretical requirement for any nonlinear macro indicator.

</details>


### [140] [What Do EEG Foundation Models Capture from Human Brain Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11410)
*Ling Tang,Qian Chen,Jilin Mei,Houshi Xu,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练学习原始信号表示，本研究发现这些模型学习的表征与手工特征之间存在显著对齐，频率域特征占主导地位，但其他特征族也有重要贡献，确认的特征能恢复模型优势的79.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究现代EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练学习的表征是否与传统手工特征(如带功率、连通性、复杂度等)对齐，这是当前未解决的重要问题。

Method: 使用三层分析方法：1）层式岭回归探测，2）LEACE式交叉协方差子空间擦除，3）透明分类器与随机特征基准对比。覆盖三个基础模型(CSBrain、CBraMod、LaBraM)、五个临床任务和63个特征的词汇表。

Result: 在945个(模型、任务、特征)单元中，68.6%具有表征因果性，21.1%仅为编码性。50个特征在至少两个任务中被所有三种架构确认为通用候选特征。频率域特征占主导，但其他五个特征族均有实质性因果贡献。确认的特征平均能恢复基础模型相对于随机基准优势的79.3%。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型学习的表征与传统手工特征存在显著对齐，特别是在较易任务中几乎完全可恢复。较难任务留下的非平凡残差为未来的概念发现指明了具体目标。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalogram (EEG) analysis rests on a hand-crafted feature catalog refined over decades, \emph{e.g.,} band power, connectivity, complexity, and more. Modern EEG foundation models bypass this catalog, learn directly from raw signals via self-supervised pretraining, and match or outperform feature-engineered baselines on most clinical benchmarks. Whether the two representations align is an open question, which we decompose into three sub-questions: \emph{what does the model learn}, \emph{what does the model use}, and \emph{how much can be explained}. We answer them with layer-wise ridge probing, LEACE-style cross-covariance subspace erasure, and a transparent classifier benchmarked against a random-feature baseline. The audit covers three foundation models (CSBrain, CBraMod, LaBraM), five clinical tasks (MDD, Stress, ISRUC-Sleep, TUSL, Siena), and a 6-family 63-feature lexicon. Of the $945$ (model, task, feature) units, $648$ ($68.6\%$) are representation-causal and $199$ ($21.1\%$) are encoded-only. Across tasks, $50$ features qualify as universal candidates with strong support (all three architectures RC) in two or more tasks. Frequency-domain features dominate, but the other five families each contribute substantial causal mass. Confirmed features recover, on average, $79.3\%$ of the foundation model's advantage over the random baseline, with a clean task gradient (MDD $\approx 0.99$ down to Stress $\approx 0.56$): tasks near ceiling are almost fully recovered by the lexicon, while harder tasks leave a non-trivial residual that pinpoints a concrete target for future concept discovery.

</details>


### [141] [Under the Hood of SKILL.md: Semantic Supply-chain Attacks on AI Agent Skill Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11418)
*Shoumik Saha,Kazem Faghih,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究SKILL.md文件中的语义供应链攻击，展示攻击者如何通过操控自然语言描述在AI智能体技能生命周期的三个阶段（发现、选择、治理）中操纵技能的可视性、选择倾向和审核结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体通过SKILL.md文件扩展能力，这些自然语言元数据和指令会影响技能的接纳、展示、选择和加载，从而引入了语义供应链风险。需要研究这种被动文档如何成为操纵智能体行为的攻击向量。

Method: 在智能体技能生命周期的三个注册表相关阶段进行SKILL.md-only攻击研究：1) 发现阶段：使用短文本触发器操纵基于嵌入的检索；2) 选择阶段：仅通过描述框架偏向功能等效的对抗变体；3) 治理阶段：使用语义规避策略避免恶意技能被阻止。使用真实ClawHub技能和现实注册机制进行实验。

Result: 1) 发现阶段：短文本触发器可显著提高对抗技能的可见性，达到86%的成对胜率和80%的Top-10排名；2) 选择阶段：仅通过描述框架，对抗变体在77.6%的配对试验中被平均选择；3) 治理阶段：语义规避策略使恶意技能在36.5%-100%的情况下避免被阻止。

Conclusion: SKILL.md不仅是被动文档，而是影响AI智能体寻找、信任和使用第三方能力的操作文本，需要重新评估其在智能体安全生态系统中的角色和防护措施。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly extend their capabilities through Agent Skills: modular filesystem packages whose SKILL.md files describe when and how agents should use them. While this design enables scalable, on-demand capability expansion, it also introduces a semantic supply-chain risk in which natural-language metadata and instructions can affect which skills are admitted, surfaced, selected, and loaded. We study SKILL.md - only attacks across three registry-facing stages of the Agent Skill lifecycle, using real ClawHub skills and realistic registry mechanisms. In Discovery, short textual triggers can manipulate embedding-based retrieval and improve adversarial skill visibility, achieving up to 86% pairwise win rate and 80% Top-10 placement. In Selection, description-only framing biases agents toward functionally equivalent adversarial variants, which are selected in 77.6% of paired trials on average. In Governance, semantic evasion strategies cause malicious skills to avoid a blocking verdict in 36.5%-100% of cases. Overall, our results show that SKILL.md is not passive documentation but operational text that shapes which third-party capabilities agents find, trust, and use.

</details>


### [142] [A Mechanistic Investigation of Supervised Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11426)
*Ruhaan Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于Sparse Autoencoder（SAE）的高分辨率诊断工具揭示：虽然SFT后LLM隐藏激活的余弦相似度很高，但通过预训练SAE投影后的稀疏潜在表示存在显著分歧，显示SFT实质上系统性地改变了特定语义特征分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到大语言模型SFT前后隐藏激活的余弦相似度很高，表面上似乎SFT对激活几何结构扰动很小。但这种现象背后可能存在未被发现的表示变化，需要更高分辨率的工具来探究SFT对模型表示的真实影响。

Method: 1. 使用在基础模型上预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为高分辨率诊断工具；2. 将SFT前后的隐藏激活通过SAE投影到稀疏潜在空间；3. 建立分析流程，机制性地研究表示分歧的驱动因素；4. 识别特定任务和特定层级的语义特征分布变化。

Result: 1. 发现SFT前后稀疏潜在表示存在显著分歧；2. 识别了任务特定和层级特定的精确语义特征分布变化模式；3. 确定了安全对齐特有的层级更新特征；4. 证明SAE能有效揭示传统相似性度量（如余弦相似度）掩盖的深层次表示变化。

Conclusion: 预训练的SAE可作为高分辨率诊断工具，揭示SFT对LLM表示的实质性影响。虽然表面激活相似度高，但稀疏潜在空间分析显示SFT系统性地改变了语义特征分布，这一发现对理解SFT机制和开发更精细的模型诊断方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: The cosine similarity between a large language model's hidden activations before and after Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) remains very high. This, at first glance, suggests that SFT leaves the model's activation geometry largely undisturbed. However, projecting both sets of activations through a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) pretrained on the base model reveals that the underlying sparse latents diverge significantly. We introduce a novel investigative pipeline which utilizes these pretrained SAEs as a high-resolution diagnostic tool to mechanistically investigate the drivers of this representational divergence. Through our analytical pipeline, we discover task-specific and layer-specific distributions of the precise semantic features that are systematically altered during supervised fine-tuning. We additionally identify a layer-wise update profile specific to safety alignment. All code, experimental scripts, and analysis files associated with this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/ruhzi/sae-investigation.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11458)
*Zihao Han,Tiangang Zhang,Huaibin Wang,Yilun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应教师曝光自蒸馏（ATESD）方法，通过动态控制教师模型在训练时能看到多少参考答案，解决了传统自蒸馏中教师过度依赖完整参考方案导致学生难以吸收的问题，显著提升了推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自蒸馏方法中，教师模型总是能看到完整的参考推理过程，这导致教师与学生在能力上存在不匹配：当教师基于远超学生当前能力的推理过程进行监督时，生成的目标标签对学生来说过于困难而难以吸收。研究表明完整曝光并非总是最优选择，且师生不匹配会随着教师看到更多特权推理而单调增长。

Method: 提出ATESD方法，将教师曝光量从固定超参数转变为可学习的训练时控制变量。使用轻量级的Beta策略控制器对曝光比例进行建模，该控制器以紧凑的训练状态统计量为条件，并为学生更新的短期保持窗口采样一个曝光值。为了使其可学习，通过折扣学习进度奖励来优化控制器，该奖励根据每个决策对学生未来改进的影响（而非即时损失变化）进行评分，解决了自蒸馏中的延迟信用分配问题。

Result: 在AIME 24、AIME 25和HMMT 25数据集上，对Qwen3-{1.7B, 4B, 8B}模型进行实验，ATESD始终优于竞争性自蒸馏和强化学习基线，相比OPSD分别提高了+0.95、+2.05和+2.33 Average@12分数，验证了自适应教师曝光作为推理自蒸馏有效新维度的价值。

Conclusion: ATESD成功解决了自蒸馏中的教师侧曝光不匹配问题，通过将曝光量作为可学习变量并采用折扣学习进度奖励进行优化，显著提升了模型的推理性能。该方法为推理自蒸馏提供了新的有效维度，具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation has become a strong recipe for LLM reasoning, where a privileged teacher supervises the student's own rollouts while conditioning on the reference solution. A design choice shared by nearly all such methods, however, has gone unquestioned: the teacher always sees the full reference reasoning. We argue that this default itself is part of the problem and identify a teacher-side exposure mismatch: when the teacher conditions on reasoning far beyond the student's current competence, the resulting token targets become too strong to absorb. A controlled fixed-exposure sweep makes this concrete on two fronts: 1) full exposure is not reliably the best choice, and 2) student-teacher mismatch grows monotonically as the teacher sees more privileged reasoning.
